"use strict";

console.log("APP JS REAL CARGADO - V37");

const API_BASE = "/api";

let documentsCache = [];
let approvalInProgress = false;


/* ================================================================
   API
================================================================ */

class ApiError extends Error {
    constructor(message, status, data = null) {
        super(message);
        this.name = "ApiError";
        this.status = status;
        this.data = data;
    }
}


function apiUrl(path) {
    const value = String(path || "");

    if (value.startsWith("/api/")) {
        return value;
    }

    if (value === "/api") {
        return value;
    }

    if (value.startsWith("/")) {
        return `${API_BASE}${value}`;
    }

    return `${API_BASE}/${value}`;
}


async function apiRequest(path, options = {}) {
    let response;

    try {
        response = await fetch(apiUrl(path), options);
    } catch (error) {
        throw new ApiError(
            "No se ha podido conectar con el servidor.",
            0,
            {
                originalError: error,
            }
        );
    }

    const contentType =
        response.headers.get("content-type") || "";

    let data = null;

    try {
        if (contentType.includes("application/json")) {
            data = await response.json();
        } else {
            const text = await response.text();
            data = text || null;
        }
    } catch (error) {
        console.error(
            "No se pudo interpretar la respuesta:",
            error
        );
    }

    if (!response.ok) {
        const detail = data?.detail ?? data;

        let message = `Error HTTP ${response.status}`;

        if (typeof detail === "string") {
            message = detail;
        } else if (detail?.message) {
            message = detail.message;
        } else if (data?.message) {
            message = data.message;
        }

        throw new ApiError(
            message,
            response.status,
            detail
        );
    }

    return data;
}


/* ================================================================
   UTILIDADES
================================================================ */

function setText(id, value) {
    const element = document.getElementById(id);

    if (element) {
        element.textContent = value ?? "";
    }
}


function escapeHtml(value) {
    return String(value ?? "")
        .replaceAll("&", "&amp;")
        .replaceAll("<", "&lt;")
        .replaceAll(">", "&gt;")
        .replaceAll('"', "&quot;")
        .replaceAll("'", "&#039;");
}


function formatMoney(value, currency = "EUR") {
    if (
        value === null ||
        value === undefined ||
        value === ""
    ) {
        return "—";
    }

    const amount = Number(value);

    if (!Number.isFinite(amount)) {
        return escapeHtml(value);
    }

    try {
        return new Intl.NumberFormat("es-ES", {
            style: "currency",
            currency: currency || "EUR",
        }).format(amount);
    } catch {
        return `${amount.toFixed(2)} ${currency || "EUR"}`;
    }
}


function formatDate(value) {
    if (!value) {
        return "—";
    }

    const date = new Date(value);

    if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) {
        return escapeHtml(value);
    }

    return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("es-ES", {
        dateStyle: "medium",
        timeStyle: "short",
    }).format(date);
}


function dateInputValue(value) {
    if (!value) {
        return "";
    }

    return String(value).slice(0, 10);
}


function normalizeDocumentsResponse(data) {
    if (Array.isArray(data)) {
        return data;
    }

    if (Array.isArray(data?.documents)) {
        return data.documents;
    }

    if (Array.isArray(data?.items)) {
        return data.items;
    }

    return [];
}


function translateStatus(status) {
    const statuses = {
        RECEIVED: "Recibido",
        PROCESSING: "Procesando",
        EXTRACTED: "Extraído",
        NEEDS_REVIEW: "Requiere revisión",
        READY_FOR_APPROVAL: "Pendiente de aprobación",
        APPROVED: "Aprobada",
        REJECTED: "Rechazada",
        FAILED: "Incidencia",
        EXPORTED: "Exportada",
    };

    return statuses[status] || status || "Pendiente";
}


function statusClassFromBackend(status) {
    const value = String(status || "").toUpperCase();

    if (["FAILED", "REJECTED"].includes(value)) {
        return "status-danger";
    }

    if (
        [
            "RECEIVED",
            "PROCESSING",
            "EXTRACTED",
            "NEEDS_REVIEW",
            "READY_FOR_APPROVAL",
        ].includes(value)
    ) {
        return "status-warning";
    }

    if (["APPROVED", "EXPORTED"].includes(value)) {
        return "status-success";
    }

    return "status-neutral";
}


function confidenceClass(confidence) {
    const number = Number(confidence || 0);

    if (number >= 90) {
        return "risk-low";
    }

    if (number >= 75) {
        return "risk-medium";
    }

    return "risk-high";
}


function requiresReview(documentItem) {
    const invoice = documentItem.invoice || {};
    const confidence = Number(invoice.confidence || 0);

    return (
        documentItem.status === "NEEDS_REVIEW" ||
        documentItem.status === "FAILED" ||
        !invoice.supplier_name ||
        !invoice.supplier_tax_id ||
        !invoice.invoice_number ||
        !invoice.invoice_date ||
        invoice.subtotal === null ||
        invoice.subtotal === undefined ||
        invoice.tax_total === null ||
        invoice.tax_total === undefined ||
        invoice.total === null ||
        invoice.total === undefined ||
        confidence < 80
    );
}


function nullableNumber(elementId) {
    const element = document.getElementById(elementId);

    if (!element) {
        return null;
    }

    const value = element.value.trim();

    if (!value) {
        return null;
    }

    const number = Number(value);

    return Number.isFinite(number) ? number : null;
}


function emptyState(icon, title, text) {
    return `
        <div class="section-empty">
            <span class="section-empty-icon">
                ${escapeHtml(icon)}
            </span>
            <div>
                <p class="section-empty-title">
                    ${escapeHtml(title)}
                </p>
                <p class="section-empty-text">
                    ${escapeHtml(text)}
                </p>
            </div>
        </div>
    `;
}


/* ================================================================
   PESTAÑAS
================================================================ */

function setupTabs() {
    const buttons = document.querySelectorAll(".nav-tab");
    const panels = document.querySelectorAll(".tab-panel");

    buttons.forEach((button) => {
        button.addEventListener("click", () => {
            const tabName = button.dataset.tab;

            buttons.forEach((item) => {
                item.classList.toggle(
                    "active",
                    item === button
                );
            });

            panels.forEach((panel) => {
                panel.classList.toggle(
                    "active",
                    panel.id === `tab-${tabName}`
                );
            });
        });
    });
}


/* ================================================================
   DOCUMENTOS
================================================================ */

async function loadDocuments() {
    try {
        const data = await apiRequest("/documents");

        documentsCache = normalizeDocumentsResponse(data);

        renderDocuments(documentsCache);
        renderInbox(documentsCache);
        renderDashboard(documentsCache);
        renderOpenRisks(documentsCache);

        return documentsCache;
    } catch (error) {
        console.error(
            "No se pudieron cargar los documentos:",
            error
        );

        renderApiError(
            "No se pudieron cargar los documentos",
            error.message
        );

        return [];
    }
}


function renderDashboard(documents) {
    const pending = documents.filter((documentItem) => {
        return [
            "RECEIVED",
            "PROCESSING",
            "EXTRACTED",
            "NEEDS_REVIEW",
            "READY_FOR_APPROVAL",
        ].includes(documentItem.status);
    });

    const risks = documents.filter(requiresReview);

    const approved = documents.filter((documentItem) => {
        return documentItem.status === "APPROVED";
    });

    const approvedTotal = approved.reduce(
        (sum, documentItem) => {
            const total = Number(
                documentItem.invoice?.total || 0
            );

            return sum + (
                Number.isFinite(total) ? total : 0
            );
        },
        0
    );

    const taxTotal = approved.reduce(
        (sum, documentItem) => {
            const tax = Number(
                documentItem.invoice?.tax_total || 0
            );

            return sum + (
                Number.isFinite(tax) ? tax : 0
            );
        },
        0
    );

    const subtotal = approved.reduce(
        (sum, documentItem) => {
            const value = Number(
                documentItem.invoice?.subtotal || 0
            );

            return sum + (
                Number.isFinite(value) ? value : 0
            );
        },
        0
    );

    setText(
        "greetingLine",
        "Resumen de documentación real"
    );
    setText("mRisks", risks.length);
    setText("mPending", pending.length);
    setText("mSpend", formatMoney(approvedTotal));
    setText("mIva", formatMoney(taxTotal));
    setText("cntFacturas", documents.length);
    setText("cntAeat", "0");
    setText("cntEmp", "0");
    setText("txCount", approved.length);
    setText("txBase", formatMoney(subtotal));
    setText("txIva", formatMoney(taxTotal));
    setText("txTotal", formatMoney(approvedTotal));
    setText("roiDocs", documents.length);
    setText("roiRisks", risks.length);
    setText("roiHours", "—");
    setText("roiCost", "—");
    setText(
        "roiNote",
        "Métricas calculadas a partir de documentos reales."
    );
}


function renderUnavailableModules() {
    const aeatList = document.getElementById("aeatList");
    const payrollList =
        document.getElementById("payrollList");
    const connectors =
        document.getElementById("connectorsList");

    setText(
        "aeatSub",
        "Módulo no habilitado en el MVP actual."
    );

    setText(
        "payrollSub",
        "Módulo no habilitado en el MVP actual."
    );

    if (aeatList) {
        aeatList.innerHTML = emptyState(
            "📭",
            "Sin integración con la AEAT",
            "Este módulo se habilitará en una fase posterior."
        );
    }

    if (payrollList) {
        payrollList.innerHTML = emptyState(
            "👥",
            "Gestión laboral no habilitada",
            "El MVP actual está centrado en facturas recibidas."
        );
    }

    if (connectors) {
        connectors.innerHTML = emptyState(
            "🔌",
            "Sin conectores activos",
            "La carga manual es la única fuente habilitada."
        );
    }
}


function renderDocuments(documents) {
    const container =
        document.getElementById("documentsList");

    if (!container) {
        return;
    }

    if (!documents.length) {
        container.innerHTML = emptyState(
            "📄",
            "Todavía no hay documentos",
            "Sube una factura PDF para comenzar."
        );
        return;
    }

    container.innerHTML = documents
        .map(realDocumentCard)
        .join("");
}


function renderInbox(documents) {
    const container =
        document.getElementById("inboxList");

    if (!container) {
        return;
    }

    if (!documents.length) {
        container.innerHTML = emptyState(
            "📥",
            "Bandeja vacía",
            "Los documentos cargados aparecerán aquí."
        );
        return;
    }

    container.innerHTML = documents
        .slice(0, 5)
        .map(realDocumentCard)
        .join("");
}


function realDocumentCard(documentItem) {
    const invoice = documentItem.invoice || {};
    const confidence = Number(invoice.confidence || 0);
    const reviewRequired = requiresReview(documentItem);
    const documentId = Number(documentItem.id);
    const invoiceId = Number(invoice.id);

    const canReview = (
        invoice.id &&
        !["APPROVED", "REJECTED"].includes(
            documentItem.status
        )
    );

    return `
        <article class="document-card ${
            reviewRequired ? "needs-review" : ""
        }">
            <div class="document-top">
                <div>
                    <p class="doc-type">
                        Factura recibida
                        ${
                            documentItem.source ===
                            "manual_upload"
                                ? `
                                    <span class="source-tag">
                                        ⬆ carga manual
                                    </span>
                                `
                                : ""
                        }
                    </p>

                    <h3>
                        ${escapeHtml(
                            invoice.supplier_name ||
                            "Proveedor sin identificar"
                        )}
                    </h3>

                    <p class="filename">
                        ${escapeHtml(
                            documentItem.original_filename ||
                            "Sin nombre"
                        )}
                    </p>
                </div>

                <div class="badges">
                    ${
                        reviewRequired
                            ? `
                                <span class="
                                    priority-pill
                                    priority-high
                                ">
                                    Revisar
                                </span>
                            `
                            : `
                                <span class="
                                    priority-pill
                                    priority-low
                                ">
                                    Normal
                                </span>
                            `
                    }

                    <span class="
                        status-pill
                        ${statusClassFromBackend(
                            documentItem.status
                        )}
                    ">
                        ${escapeHtml(
                            translateStatus(
                                documentItem.status
                            )
                        )}
                    </span>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div class="document-grid">
                <div>
                    <span>Número</span>
                    <strong>
                        ${escapeHtml(
                            invoice.invoice_number || "—"
                        )}
                    </strong>
                </div>

                <div>
                    <span>Fecha</span>
                    <strong>
                        ${escapeHtml(
                            invoice.invoice_date || "—"
                        )}
                    </strong>
                </div>

                <div>
                    <span>Importe</span>
                    <strong>
                        ${formatMoney(
                            invoice.total,
                            invoice.currency
                        )}
                    </strong>
                </div>

                <div>
                    <span>Confianza</span>
                    <strong class="${
                        confidenceClass(confidence)
                    }">
                        ${confidence}%
                    </strong>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div class="card-actions">
                <button
                    class="btn-ghost"
                    type="button"
                    onclick="showDetail(${documentId})"
                >
                    Ver detalle
                </button>

                <a
                    class="btn-ghost"
                    href="/api/documents/${documentId}/file"
                    target="_blank"
                    rel="noopener noreferrer"
                >
                    Abrir archivo
                </a>

                ${
                    canReview
                        ? `
                            <button
                                class="act-btn act-primary"
                                type="button"
                                onclick="
                                    approveInvoice(
                                        ${invoiceId},
                                        ${documentId},
                                        this
                                    )
                                "
                            >
                                Aprobar
                            </button>

                            <button
                                class="act-btn act-danger"
                                type="button"
                                onclick="
                                    rejectInvoice(
                                        ${invoiceId},
                                        ${documentId}
                                    )
                                "
                            >
                                Rechazar
                            </button>
                        `
                        : ""
                }
            </div>
        </article>
    `;
}


/* ================================================================
   CARGA
================================================================ */

function setupUpload() {
    const fileInput =
        document.getElementById("fileInput");

    if (!fileInput) {
        console.warn("No se encontró #fileInput.");
        return;
    }

    fileInput.addEventListener("change", async () => {
        if (fileInput.files?.length) {
            await uploadSelectedFile(fileInput);
        }
    });
}


async function uploadSelectedFile(fileInput) {
    const file = fileInput.files?.[0];

    if (!file) {
        showMessage(
            "Selecciona primero un archivo.",
            "warning"
        );
        return;
    }

    const lowerName = file.name.toLowerCase();

    if (
        !lowerName.endsWith(".pdf") &&
        !lowerName.endsWith(".txt")
    ) {
        showMessage(
            "El archivo debe ser PDF o TXT.",
            "error"
        );
        fileInput.value = "";
        return;
    }

    const maxBytes = 20 * 1024 * 1024;

    if (file.size > maxBytes) {
        showMessage(
            "El archivo supera el límite de 20 MB.",
            "error"
        );
        fileInput.value = "";
        return;
    }

    try {
        showMessage(
            `Procesando "${file.name}"…`,
            "info"
        );

        const formData = new FormData();

        formData.append(
            "uploaded_file",
            file,
            file.name
        );

        const result = await apiRequest("/upload", {
            method: "POST",
            body: formData,
        });

        showMessage(
            result.message ||
            `Documento "${file.name}" procesado.`,
            result.duplicate ? "warning" : "success"
        );

        fileInput.value = "";

        await loadDocuments();

        const uploadedDocumentId = result?.document?.id;

        if (uploadedDocumentId) {
            await showDetail(uploadedDocumentId);
        }
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("Error al subir:", error);

        showMessage(
            `No se pudo procesar el archivo: ${
                error.message
            }`,
            "error"
        );

        fileInput.value = "";
    }
}


/* ================================================================
   DETALLE
================================================================ */

function ensureDetailDialog() {
    let dialog = document.getElementById(
        "documentDetailDialog"
    );

    if (dialog) {
        return dialog;
    }

    dialog = document.createElement("dialog");
    dialog.id = "documentDetailDialog";
    dialog.className = "document-detail-dialog";

    dialog.innerHTML = `
        <div class="detail-dialog-header">
            <h2>Detalle de factura</h2>

            <button
                type="button"
                class="btn-ghost"
                id="closeDetailDialog"
            >
                ✕ Cerrar
            </button>
        </div>

        <div id="documentDetailContent"></div>
    `;

    document.body.appendChild(dialog);

    document
        .getElementById("closeDetailDialog")
        ?.addEventListener(
            "click",
            () => dialog.close()
        );

    dialog.addEventListener("click", (event) => {
        if (event.target === dialog) {
            dialog.close();
        }
    });

    return dialog;
}


async function showDetail(documentId) {
    const dialog = ensureDetailDialog();
    const content = document.getElementById(
        "documentDetailContent"
    );

    content.innerHTML = "<p>Cargando documento…</p>";

    if (!dialog.open) {
        dialog.showModal();
    }

    try {
        const documentItem = await apiRequest(
            `/documents/${documentId}`
        );

        const invoice = documentItem.invoice || {};
        const invoiceId = Number(invoice.id);
        const canReview = (
            invoice.id &&
            !["APPROVED", "REJECTED"].includes(
                documentItem.status
            )
        );

        content.innerHTML = `
            <div class="detail-grid">
                <label>
                    Proveedor
                    <input
                        id="detailSupplierName"
                        data-invoice-field="supplier_name"
                        type="text"
                        value="${escapeHtml(
                            invoice.supplier_name || ""
                        )}"
                    >
                </label>

                <label>
                    NIF proveedor
                    <input
                        id="detailSupplierTaxId"
                        data-invoice-field="supplier_tax_id"
                        type="text"
                        value="${escapeHtml(
                            invoice.supplier_tax_id || ""
                        )}"
                    >
                </label>

                <label>
                    Número de factura
                    <input
                        id="detailInvoiceNumber"
                        data-invoice-field="invoice_number"
                        type="text"
                        value="${escapeHtml(
                            invoice.invoice_number || ""
                        )}"
                    >
                </label>

                <label>
                    Fecha de factura
                    <input
                        id="detailInvoiceDate"
                        data-invoice-field="invoice_date"
                        type="date"
                        value="${escapeHtml(
                            dateInputValue(
                                invoice.invoice_date
                            )
                        )}"
                    >
                </label>

                <label>
                    Base imponible
                    <input
                        id="detailSubtotal"
                        data-invoice-field="subtotal"
                        type="number"
                        step="0.01"
                        value="${escapeHtml(
                            invoice.subtotal ?? ""
                        )}"
                    >
                </label>

                <label>
                    IVA
                    <input
                        id="detailTaxTotal"
                        data-invoice-field="tax_total"
                        type="number"
                        step="0.01"
                        value="${escapeHtml(
                            invoice.tax_total ?? ""
                        )}"
                    >
                </label>

                <label>
                    Total
                    <input
                        id="detailTotal"
                        data-invoice-field="total"
                        type="number"
                        step="0.01"
                        value="${escapeHtml(
                            invoice.total ?? ""
                        )}"
                    >
                </label>

                <label>
                    Moneda
                    <input
                        id="detailCurrency"
                        data-invoice-field="currency"
                        type="text"
                        maxlength="3"
                        value="${escapeHtml(
                            invoice.currency || "EUR"
                        )}"
                    >
                </label>
            </div>

            <div class="detail-summary">
                <p>
                    <strong>Estado:</strong>
                    ${escapeHtml(
                        translateStatus(
                            documentItem.status
                        )
                    )}
                </p>

                <p>
                    <strong>Confianza:</strong>
                    ${Number(invoice.confidence || 0)}%
                </p>

                <p>
                    <strong>Archivo:</strong>
                    ${escapeHtml(
                        documentItem.original_filename
                    )}
                </p>

                <p>
                    <strong>Creado:</strong>
                    ${formatDate(
                        documentItem.created_at
                    )}
                </p>
            </div>

            <div class="card-actions">
                ${
                    invoice.id
                        ? `
                            <button
                                type="button"
                                class="act-btn act-primary"
                                onclick="
                                    saveInvoice(
                                        ${invoiceId},
                                        ${Number(
                                            documentItem.id
                                        )}
                                    )
                                "
                            >
                                Guardar correcciones
                            </button>
                        `
                        : ""
                }

                ${
                    canReview
                        ? `
                            <button
                                type="button"
                                class="act-btn act-primary"
                                onclick="
                                    approveInvoice(
                                        ${invoiceId},
                                        ${Number(
                                            documentItem.id
                                        )},
                                        this
                                    )
                                "
                            >
                                Aprobar
                            </button>

                            <button
                                type="button"
                                class="act-btn act-danger"
                                onclick="
                                    rejectInvoice(
                                        ${invoiceId},
                                        ${Number(
                                            documentItem.id
                                        )}
                                    )
                                "
                            >
                                Rechazar
                            </button>
                        `
                        : ""
                }

                <button
                    type="button"
                    class="btn-ghost"
                    onclick="
                        reprocessDocument(
                            ${Number(documentItem.id)}
                        )
                    "
                >
                    Reprocesar
                </button>

                <a
                    class="btn-ghost"
                    href="/api/documents/${Number(
                        documentItem.id
                    )}/file"
                    target="_blank"
                    rel="noopener noreferrer"
                >
                    Abrir archivo
                </a>
            </div>
        `;
    } catch (error) {
        content.innerHTML = emptyState(
            "⚠️",
            "No se pudo abrir el documento",
            error.message
        );
    }
}


/* ================================================================
   REVISIÓN
================================================================ */

async function saveInvoice(invoiceId, documentId) {
    const body = {
        supplier_name:
            document
                .getElementById("detailSupplierName")
                ?.value.trim() || null,

        supplier_tax_id:
            document
                .getElementById("detailSupplierTaxId")
                ?.value.trim() || null,

        invoice_number:
            document
                .getElementById("detailInvoiceNumber")
                ?.value.trim() || null,

        invoice_date:
            document
                .getElementById("detailInvoiceDate")
                ?.value || null,

        subtotal: nullableNumber("detailSubtotal"),
        tax_total: nullableNumber("detailTaxTotal"),
        total: nullableNumber("detailTotal"),

        currency:
            document
                .getElementById("detailCurrency")
                ?.value.trim()
                .toUpperCase() || "EUR",
    };

    try {
        await apiRequest(`/invoices/${invoiceId}`, {
            method: "PATCH",
            headers: {
                "Content-Type": "application/json",
            },
            body: JSON.stringify(body),
        });

        showMessage(
            "Correcciones guardadas correctamente.",
            "success"
        );

        await loadDocuments();
        await showDetail(documentId);
    } catch (error) {
        showMessage(
            `No se pudieron guardar los cambios: ${
                error.message
            }`,
            "error"
        );
    }
}


function formatMissingFields(missingFields) {
    if (
        !Array.isArray(missingFields) ||
        !missingFields.length
    ) {
        return "• Campos sin identificar";
    }

    return missingFields
        .map((item) => {
            return `• ${
                item?.label ||
                item?.field ||
                "Campo desconocido"
            }`;
        })
        .join("\n");
}


function highlightMissingFields(missingFields) {
    document
        .querySelectorAll("[data-invoice-field]")
        .forEach((element) => {
            element.classList.remove("field-missing");
            element.removeAttribute("aria-invalid");
        });

    if (!Array.isArray(missingFields)) {
        return;
    }

    let firstElement = null;

    missingFields.forEach((item) => {
        const fieldName = item?.field;

        if (!fieldName) {
            return;
        }

        const element = document.querySelector(
            `[data-invoice-field="${fieldName}"]`
        );

        if (!element) {
            return;
        }

        element.classList.add("field-missing");
        element.setAttribute("aria-invalid", "true");

        if (!firstElement) {
            firstElement = element;
        }
    });

    if (firstElement) {
        firstElement.scrollIntoView({
            behavior: "smooth",
            block: "center",
        });

        firstElement.focus();
    }
}


async function sendApproval(invoiceId, force) {
    return apiRequest(
        `/invoices/${invoiceId}/approve`,
        {
            method: "POST",
            headers: {
                "Content-Type": "application/json",
            },
            body: JSON.stringify({
                force: Boolean(force),
            }),
        }
    );
}


async function approveInvoice(
    invoiceId,
    documentId = null,
    button = null
) {
    if (!invoiceId) {
        showMessage(
            "No se ha encontrado el identificador de la factura.",
            "error"
        );
        return;
    }

    if (approvalInProgress) {
        return;
    }

    approvalInProgress = true;

    const originalText = button?.textContent || "Aprobar";

    if (button) {
        button.disabled = true;
        button.textContent = "Comprobando…";
    }

    try {
        const result = await sendApproval(
            invoiceId,
            false
        );

        showMessage(
            result.message ||
            "Factura aprobada correctamente.",
            result.approved_with_warnings
                ? "warning"
                : "success"
        );

        await loadDocuments();

        if (documentId) {
            await showDetail(documentId);
        }
    } catch (error) {
        const detail = error.data || {};

        const isMissingFieldsWarning = (
            error instanceof ApiError &&
            error.status === 409 &&
            detail.code === "MISSING_FIELDS"
        );

        if (isMissingFieldsWarning) {
            const missingFields =
                detail.missing_fields || [];

            const fieldsText =
                formatMissingFields(missingFields);

            const approveAnyway = window.confirm(
                `${
                    detail.message ||
                    "La factura está incompleta."
                }\n\n` +
                `Campos pendientes:\n${fieldsText}\n\n` +
                "Aceptar: aprobar con advertencias.\n" +
                "Cancelar: volver para completar los campos."
            );

            if (!approveAnyway) {
                showMessage(
                    "La factura no se ha aprobado. " +
                    "Puedes completar los campos pendientes.",
                    "info"
                );

                if (documentId) {
                    await showDetail(documentId);

                    window.setTimeout(() => {
                        highlightMissingFields(
                            missingFields
                        );
                    }, 100);
                }

                return;
            }

            if (button) {
                button.textContent =
                    "Aprobando con advertencias…";
            }

            try {
                const forcedResult = await sendApproval(
                    invoiceId,
                    true
                );

                showMessage(
                    forcedResult.message ||
                    "Factura aprobada con advertencias.",
                    "warning"
                );

                await loadDocuments();

                if (documentId) {
                    await showDetail(documentId);
                }
            } catch (forcedError) {
                console.error(
                    "Error en aprobación forzada:",
                    forcedError
                );

                showMessage(
                    forcedError.message ||
                    "No se pudo aprobar la factura.",
                    "error"
                );
            }

            return;
        }

        console.error("Error al aprobar:", error);

        showMessage(
            error.message ||
            "No se pudo aprobar la factura.",
            "error"
        );
    } finally {
        approvalInProgress = false;

        if (button?.isConnected) {
            button.disabled = false;
            button.textContent = originalText;
        }
    }
}


async function rejectInvoice(invoiceId, documentId) {
    const reason = window.prompt(
        "Indica el motivo del rechazo:"
    );

    if (reason === null) {
        return;
    }

    if (!reason.trim()) {
        showMessage(
            "Debes indicar un motivo.",
            "warning"
        );
        return;
    }

    try {
        await apiRequest(
            `/invoices/${invoiceId}/reject`,
            {
                method: "POST",
                headers: {
                    "Content-Type": "application/json",
                },
                body: JSON.stringify({
                    reason: reason.trim(),
                }),
            }
        );

        showMessage(
            "Factura rechazada.",
            "success"
        );

        await loadDocuments();

        const dialog = document.getElementById(
            "documentDetailDialog"
        );

        if (dialog?.open && documentId) {
            await showDetail(documentId);
        }
    } catch (error) {
        showMessage(
            `No se pudo rechazar: ${error.message}`,
            "error"
        );
    }
}


async function reprocessDocument(documentId) {
    const confirmed = window.confirm(
        "¿Quieres volver a ejecutar la extracción?"
    );

    if (!confirmed) {
        return;
    }

    try {
        const result = await apiRequest(
            `/documents/${documentId}/reprocess`,
            {
                method: "POST",
            }
        );

        showMessage(
            result.message || "Documento reprocesado.",
            result.success ? "success" : "error"
        );

        await loadDocuments();
        await showDetail(documentId);
    } catch (error) {
        showMessage(
            `No se pudo reprocesar: ${error.message}`,
            "error"
        );
    }
}


/* ================================================================
   RIESGOS Y MENSAJES
================================================================ */

function renderOpenRisks(documents) {
    const panel =
        document.getElementById("openRisksPanel");
    const text =
        document.getElementById("openRisksText");
    const list =
        document.getElementById("openRisksList");

    if (!panel || !text || !list) {
        return;
    }

    const risks = documents.filter(requiresReview);

    if (!risks.length) {
        panel.classList.add("hidden");
        list.innerHTML = "";
        return;
    }

    panel.classList.remove("hidden");

    text.textContent =
        `${risks.length} documento(s) requieren revisión.`;

    list.innerHTML = risks
        .map((documentItem) => {
            const invoice = documentItem.invoice || {};

            return `
                <div class="risk-row">
                    <div>
                        <strong>
                            ${escapeHtml(
                                invoice.supplier_name ||
                                "Proveedor sin identificar"
                            )}
                        </strong>

                        <span>
                            ${formatMoney(
                                invoice.total,
                                invoice.currency
                            )}
                        </span>
                    </div>

                    <button
                        type="button"
                        class="btn-ghost"
                        onclick="
                            showDetail(
                                ${Number(documentItem.id)}
                            )
                        "
                    >
                        Revisar
                    </button>
                </div>
            `;
        })
        .join("");
}


function showMessage(message, type = "info") {
    let container =
        document.getElementById("appMessages");

    if (!container) {
        container = document.createElement("div");
        container.id = "appMessages";
        container.className = "app-messages";
        document.body.appendChild(container);
    }

    const item = document.createElement("div");

    item.className =
        `app-message app-message-${type}`;

    item.textContent = message;

    container.appendChild(item);

    window.setTimeout(() => {
        item.remove();
    }, 6000);
}


function renderApiError(title, detail) {
    const containers = [
        document.getElementById("documentsList"),
        document.getElementById("inboxList"),
    ].filter(Boolean);

    if (!containers.length) {
        showMessage(
            `${title}: ${detail}`,
            "error"
        );
        return;
    }

    containers.forEach((container) => {
        container.innerHTML = emptyState(
            "⚠️",
            title,
            detail
        );
    });
}


/* ================================================================
   FUNCIONES GLOBALES PARA LOS BOTONES DINÁMICOS
================================================================ */

window.showDetail = showDetail;
window.saveInvoice = saveInvoice;
window.approveInvoice = approveInvoice;
window.rejectInvoice = rejectInvoice;
window.reprocessDocument = reprocessDocument;

   INICIALIZACIÓN

        console.log(
            "Inicializando aplicación real V37"
        );

        setupTabs();
        setupUpload();
        renderUnavailableModules();

        await loadDocuments();
    }
);

   63: def orm_to_dict(instance: Any) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
   64:     """
   65:     Convierte un modelo SQLAlchemy en un diccionario serializable.
   66: 
   67:     Solo incluye columnas reales y excluye relaciones ORM para evitar
   68:     referencias circulares como document.invoice.document.
   69:     """
   70:     if instance is None:
   71:         return None
   72: 
   73:     mapper = sqlalchemy_inspect(instance).mapper
   74: 
   75:     return {
   76:         attribute.key: getattr(instance, attribute.key)
   77:         for attribute in mapper.column_attrs
   78:     }

  179: def normalize_actor(actor: str | None) -> str:
  180:     if not actor:
  181:         return "usuario-local"
  182: 
  183:     cleaned = SAFE_ACTOR_PATTERN.sub("", actor).strip()
  184: 
  185:     if not cleaned:
  186:         return "usuario-local"
  187: 
  188:     return cleaned[:100]
  189: 
  190: 
  191: def clean_original_filename(filename: str | None) -> str:
  192:     if not filename:
  193:         return "documento-sin-nombre"
  194: 
  195:     cleaned = Path(filename).name.strip()
  196: 
  197:     if not cleaned:
  198:         return "documento-sin-nombre"
  199: 
  200:     return cleaned[:255]

  203: def get_document_file_path(document: Document) -> Path:
  204:     upload_directory = settings.upload_dir.resolve()
  205: 
  206:     file_path = (
  207:         upload_directory / document.stored_filename
  208:     ).resolve()
  209: 
  210:     try:
  211:         file_path.relative_to(upload_directory)
  212:     except ValueError as error:
  213:         raise HTTPException(
  214:             status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
  215:             detail="La ruta almacenada para el documento no es válida.",
  216:         ) from error
  217: 
  218:     return file_path
  219: 
  220: 
  221: def validate_file_signature(
  222:     *,
  223:     file_path: Path,
  224:     extension: str,
  225: ) -> None:
  226:     if extension == ".pdf":
  227:         with file_path.open("rb") as file_handle:
  228:             signature = file_handle.read(5)
  229: 
  230:         if signature != b"%PDF-":
  231:             raise HTTPException(
  232:                 status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
  233:                 detail=(
  234:                     "El archivo tiene extensión PDF, pero su contenido "
  235:                     "no corresponde a un PDF válido."
  236:                 ),
  237:             )
  238: 
  239:         return
  240: 
  241:     if extension == ".txt":
  242:         with file_path.open("rb") as file_handle:
  243:             sample = file_handle.read(8192)
  244: 
  245:         if b"\x00" in sample:
  246:             raise HTTPException(
  247:                 status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
  248:                 detail=(
  249:                     "El archivo TXT contiene datos binarios y no puede "
  250:                     "procesarse como texto."
  251:                 ),
  252:             )
  253: 
  254:         return
  255: 
  256:     raise HTTPException(
  257:         status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
  258:         detail=f"El tipo de archivo {extension} no está permitido.",
  259:     )
  260: 
  261: 
  262: def response_media_type(document: Document) -> str:
  263:     if document.extension == ".pdf":
  264:         return "application/pdf"
  265: 
  266:     if document.extension == ".txt":
  267:         return "text/plain; charset=utf-8"
  268: 
  269:     return "application/octet-stream"

  272: def check_invoice_is_editable(invoice: Invoice) -> None:
  273:     if invoice.review_status == "APPROVED":
  274:         raise HTTPException(
  275:             status_code=status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT,
  276:             detail=(
  277:                 "La factura ya está aprobada y no puede modificarse "
  278:                 "con esta operación."
  279:             ),
  280:         )
  281: 
  282: def get_missing_invoice_fields(
  283:     invoice: Invoice,
  284: ) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
  285:     """
  286:     Obtiene los campos importantes que todavía no tienen valor.
  287: 
  288:     El número 0 no se considera vacío porque puede ser válido en una
  289:     factura exenta, sin IVA o con importes concretos a cero.
  290:     """
  291: 
  292:     fields = (
  293:         (
  294:             "supplier_name",
  295:             "Nombre del proveedor",
  296:         ),
  297:         (
  298:             "supplier_tax_id",
  299:             "NIF/CIF del proveedor",
  300:         ),
  301:         (
  302:             "invoice_number",
  303:             "Número de factura",
  304:         ),
  305:         (
  306:             "invoice_date",
  307:             "Fecha de factura",
  308:         ),
  309:         (
  310:             "subtotal",
  311:             "Base imponible",
  312:         ),
  313:         (
  314:             "tax_total",
  315:             "Importe de IVA",
  316:         ),
  317:         (
  318:             "total",
  319:             "Importe total",
  320:         ),
  321:         (
  322:             "currency",
  323:             "Moneda",
  324:         ),
  325:     )
  326: 
  327:     missing_fields: list[dict[str, str]] = []
  328: 
  329:     for field_name, label in fields:
  330:         value = getattr(invoice, field_name, None)
  331: 
  332:         is_missing = value is None
  333: 
  334:         if isinstance(value, str):
  335:             is_missing = not value.strip()
  336: 
  337:         if is_missing:
  338:             missing_fields.append(
  339:                 {
  340:                     "field": field_name,
  341:                     "label": label,
  342:                 }
  343:             )
  344: 
  345:     return missing_fields
  346: 
  347: # -------------------------------------------------------------------
  348: # Estado de la API
  349: # -------------------------------------------------------------------

  360:         "database": (
  361:             "sqlite"
  362:             if settings.database_url.startswith("sqlite")
  363:             else "external"
  364:         ),
  365:         "demo_connectors_enabled": settings.enable_demo_connectors,
  366:     }

  368: 
  369: # -------------------------------------------------------------------
  370: # Carga manual
  371: # -------------------------------------------------------------------
  372: 
  373: @app.post(
  374:     "/api/upload",
  375:     response_model=UploadResponse,
  376:     status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
  377:     tags=["Documentos"],
  378: )
  379: async def upload_document(
  380:     database: DatabaseDependency,
  381:     uploaded_file: UploadFile = File(...),
  382:     actor_header: ActorHeader = None,
  383: ) -> UploadResponse:
  384:     actor = normalize_actor(actor_header)
  385: 
  386:     original_filename = clean_original_filename(
  387:         uploaded_file.filename
  388:     )
  389: 
  390:     extension = Path(original_filename).suffix.lower()
  391: 
  392:     if not extension:
  393:         raise HTTPException(
  394:             status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
  395:             detail="El archivo no tiene extensión.",
  396:         )
  397: 
  398:     if extension not in settings.allowed_extension_set:
  399:         allowed = ", ".join(
  400:             sorted(settings.allowed_extension_set)
  401:         )
  402: 
  403:         raise HTTPException(
  404:             status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
  405:             detail=(
  406:                 f"Extensión no permitida: {extension}. "
  407:                 f"Extensiones permitidas: {allowed}."
  408:             ),
  409:         )
  410: 
  411:     content_type = uploaded_file.content_type
  412: 
  413:     temporary_filename = (
  414:         f".upload-{uuid.uuid4().hex}.tmp"
  415:     )
  416: 
  417:     temporary_path = (
  418:         settings.upload_dir / temporary_filename
  419:     )
  420: 
  421:     sha256 = hashlib.sha256()
  422:     total_size = 0
  423: 
  424:     try:
  425:         with temporary_path.open("wb") as destination:
  426:             while True:
  427:                 chunk = await uploaded_file.read(
  428:                     UPLOAD_CHUNK_SIZE
  429:                 )
  430: 
  431:                 if not chunk:
  432:                     break
  433: 
  434:                 total_size += len(chunk)
  435: 
  436:                 if total_size > settings.max_upload_size:
  437:                     raise HTTPException(
  438:                         status_code=(
  439:                             status.HTTP_413_REQUEST_ENTITY_TOO_LARGE
  440:                         ),
  441:                         detail=(
  442:                             "El archivo supera el tamaño máximo "
  443:                             f"permitido de "
  444:                             f"{settings.max_upload_size} bytes."
  445:                         ),
  446:                     )
  447: 
  448:                 sha256.update(chunk)
  449:                 destination.write(chunk)
  450: 
  451:     except Exception:
  452:         temporary_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
  453:         raise
  454: 
  455:     finally:
  456:         await uploaded_file.close()
  457: 
  458:     if total_size == 0:
  459:         temporary_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
  460: 
  461:         raise HTTPException(
  462:             status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
  463:             detail="El archivo está vacío.",
  464:         )
  465: 
  466:     try:
  467:         validate_file_signature(
  468:             file_path=temporary_path,
  469:             extension=extension,
  470:         )
  471:     except Exception:
  472:         temporary_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
  473:         raise
  474: 
  475:     file_hash = sha256.hexdigest()
  476: 
  477:     duplicate_statement = (
  478:         select(Document.id)
  479:         .where(Document.sha256 == file_hash)
  480:         .limit(1)
  481:     )
  482: 
  483:     duplicate_document_id = database.scalar(
  484:         duplicate_statement
  485:     )
  486: 
  487:     if duplicate_document_id is not None:
  488:         temporary_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
  489: 
  490:         duplicate_document = get_document(
  491:             database,
  492:             duplicate_document_id,
  493:         )
  494: 
  495:         if duplicate_document is None:
  496:             raise HTTPException(
  497:                 status_code=status.HTTP_500_INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,
  498:                 detail=(
  499:                     "Se detectó un documento duplicado, "
  500:                     "pero no pudo recuperarse."
  501:                 ),
  502:             )
  503: 
  504:         add_audit_event(
  505:             database,
  506:             action="document.duplicate_upload_attempt",
  507:             entity_type="document",
  508:             entity_id=duplicate_document.id,
  509:             actor=actor,
  510:             event_data={
  511:                 "attempted_filename": original_filename,
  512:                 "sha256": file_hash,
  513:                 "size_bytes": total_size,
  514:             },
  515:         )
  516:         database.commit()
  517: 
  518:         # Si la factura no está aprobada, vuelve a ejecutar la extracción.
  519:         can_reprocess = (
  520:             duplicate_document.invoice is None
  521:             or duplicate_document.invoice.review_status
  522:             != "APPROVED"
  523:         )
  524: 
  525:         if can_reprocess:
  526:             duplicate_file_path = get_document_file_path(
  527:                 duplicate_document
  528:             )
  529: 
  530:             if duplicate_file_path.is_file():
  531:                 try:
  532:                     process_document(
  533:                         database,
  534:                         document=duplicate_document,
  535:                         file_path=duplicate_file_path,
  536:                         actor=actor,
  537:                     )
  538:                 except Exception as error:
  539:                     database.rollback()
  540: 
  541:                     recovered_document = get_document(
  542:                         database,
  543:                         duplicate_document.id,
  544:                     )
  545: 
  546:                     return UploadResponse(
  547:                         success=True,
  548:                         duplicate=True,
  549:                         message=(
  550:                             "El archivo ya existía y se intentó "
  551:                             "reprocesar, pero la extracción falló. "
  552:                             f"Error: {error}"
  553:                         ),
  554:                         document=recovered_document,
  555:                     )
  556: 
  557:         refreshed_document = get_document(
  558:             database,
  559:             duplicate_document.id,
  560:         )
  561: 
  562:         return UploadResponse(
  563:             success=True,
  564:             duplicate=True,
  565:             message=(
  566:                 "El archivo ya existía y se ha vuelto "
  567:                 "a procesar correctamente."
  568:                 if can_reprocess
  569:                 else (
  570:                     "El archivo ya existía. No se ha reprocesado "
  571:                     "porque la factura está aprobada."
  572:                 )
  573:             ),
  574:             document=refreshed_document,
  575:         )
  576: 
  577: 
  578: # -------------------------------------------------------------------
  579: # Listado
  580: # -------------------------------------------------------------------
  581: 
  582: @app.get(
  583:     "/api/documents",
  584:     response_model=list[DocumentListItem],
  585:     tags=["Documentos"],
  586: )
  587: def list_documents(
  588:     database: DatabaseDependency,
  589:     document_status: str | None = Query(
  590:         default=None,
  591:         alias="status",
  592:     ),
  593:     source: str | None = Query(default=None),
  594:     is_demo: bool | None = Query(default=None),
  595:     offset: int = Query(default=0, ge=0),
  596:     limit: int = Query(default=50, ge=1, le=200),
  597: ) -> list[Document]:
  598:     statement = (
  599:         select(Document)
  600:         .options(
  601:             selectinload(Document.invoice).selectinload(
  602:                 Invoice.tax_lines
  603:             )
  604:         )
  605:         .order_by(
  606:             Document.created_at.desc(),
  607:             Document.id.desc(),
  608:         )
  609:         .offset(offset)
  610:         .limit(limit)
  611:     )
  612: 
  613:     if document_status:
  614:         statement = statement.where(
  615:             Document.status
  616:             == document_status.strip().upper()
  617:         )
  618: 
  619:     if source:
  620:         statement = statement.where(
  621:             Document.source == source.strip()
  622:         )
  623: 
  624:     if is_demo is not None:
  625:         statement = statement.where(
  626:             Document.is_demo.is_(is_demo)
  627:         )
  628: 
  629:     documents = database.scalars(statement).all()
  630: 
  631:     return list(documents)
  632: 
  633:

  638: @app.get(
  639:     "/api/documents/{document_id}",
  640:     response_model=DocumentDetail,
  641:     tags=["Documentos"],
  642: )
  643: def document_detail(
  644:     document_id: int,
  645:     database: DatabaseDependency,
  646: ) -> Document:
  647:     document = get_document(
  648:         database,
  649:         document_id,
  650:     )
  651: 
  652:     if document is None:
  653:         raise HTTPException(
  654:             status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
  655:             detail="Documento no encontrado.",
  656:         )
  657: 
  658:     return document
  659:

  661: # -------------------------------------------------------------------
  662: # Archivo original
  663: # -------------------------------------------------------------------
  664: 
  665: @app.get(
  666:     "/api/documents/{document_id}/file",
  667:     response_class=FileResponse,
  668:     tags=["Documentos"],
  669: )
  670: def document_file(
  671:     document_id: int,
  672:     database: DatabaseDependency,
  673: ) -> FileResponse:
  674:     document = get_document(
  675:         database,
  676:         document_id,
  677:     )
  678: 
  679:     if document is None:
  680:         raise HTTPException(
  681:             status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
  682:             detail="Documento no encontrado.",
  683:         )
  684: 
  685:     file_path = get_document_file_path(document)
  686: 
  687:     if not file_path.is_file():
  688:         raise HTTPException(
  689:             status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
  690:             detail=(
  691:                 "El registro existe, pero el archivo original "
  692:                 "no se encuentra en el disco."
  693:             ),
  694:         )
  695: 
  696:     return FileResponse(
  697:         path=file_path,
  698:         media_type=response_media_type(document),
  699:         filename=document.original_filename,
  700:         content_disposition_type="inline",
  701:     )
